Our framework achieves a 92.5% symbolic coverage of isoniazid-resistant predictions and effectively identifies MDR co-occurrence artifacts by issuing laboratory follow-up flags for 'UNCERTAIN' cases. We demonstrate that neuro-symbolic grounding provides a verifiable audit trail for clinicians, bridging the gap between predictive accuracy and clinical trust.

</details>


### [74] [Necessary but Not Sufficient: Temperature Control and Reproducibility in LLM-as-Judge Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26185)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM-as-judge评估中温度参数设置导致判决结果不可重复，即使强制贪心解码也无法完全消除波动，建议将评估者分歧作为核心健康指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑"温度=0使评估确定性"这一普遍假设，揭示安全评估管线中判决结果不可重复的结构性缺陷。

Method: 基于Japan AISI的开源安全评估代码库aisev，跨两家API提供商、三个模型层级、五种采样配置进行690次API调用实验，分析温度与种子参数对判决一致性的影响。

Result: 默认温度1.0时边界样本跨运行不一致率达~50%；强制温度=0且top_k=1仍无法完全消除波动（7个边界样本中1-2个不可重复）；Claude Opus 4.7/4.8已弃用温度参数。

Conclusion: 评估框架应将评估者分歧作为一阶健康指标纳入报告，而非仅汇报单次判决结果。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge ("grader") components are now standard in evaluation harnesses, including safety evaluations where a pass/fail verdict may gate downstream deployment decisions. A widespread assumption is that setting the grader's sampling temperature to 0 makes grading deterministic. We test this assumption against a real safety-evaluation codebase (Japan AISI's open-source aisev) and show it fails on two levels. First, the harness invokes its grader without setting temperature or seed; the underlying provider silently applies its default of 1.0, so items near the decision boundary flip pass/fail across identical runs (per-item disagreement up to ~50% over 20 runs). Second, pinning temperature=0 reduces but does not eliminate flips: across 690 API calls spanning two providers, three model tiers, and five sampling configurations, 1-2 of 7 borderline items remain non-reproducible even under forced greedy decoding (top_k=1). Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 has since deprecated temperature entirely, rendering the primary mitigation inapplicable to newer model generations. These findings expose a structural gap: evaluation harnesses that report single-run verdicts without variance or grader-disagreement metrics can present noise as a safety property. We release a reproduction harness (690 calls, 7 conditions) and recommend that harnesses treat grader disagreement as a first-class health metric alongside the scores themselves.

</details>


### [75] [Clue-Guided Money Laundering Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26189)
*Boyang Wang,Jianing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Clue2Group框架，通过线索引导的渐进式图挖掘方法，从初始线索出发逐步恢复洗钱团伙的完整结构，解决了现有图异常检测方法仅产生节点级告警、无法匹配真实反洗钱调查流程的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法主要产生节点级风险告警，而全局团伙发现方法被动搜索整个网络，两者均与真实反洗钱调查流程不匹配。实际调查中，分析师通常从具体线索出发，逐步扩大调查范围以恢复犯罪团伙，因此需要一种线索引导的团伙发现方法。

Method: 提出Clue2Group框架：首先构建紧凑的局部调查上下文以降低噪声并保留链状和环状洗钱结构；然后使用多语义局部时序GNN估计线索条件化的局部风险场；最后整合风险特征、结构证据和先验模式来恢复一致的洗钱团伙。

Result: 在两个大规模反洗钱基准数据集上的实验表明，Clue2Group为反洗钱调查提供了实用的线索驱动分析框架。

Conclusion: Clue2Group是弥合基于图的反洗钱研究与实际调查工作流程之间差距的可行步骤，为线索驱动的团伙发现提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Money Laundering Group Discovery (MLGD) aims to identify hidden criminal groups and recover their complete structures in large-scale financial networks. Existing graph anomaly detection methods mainly produce node-level risk alerts, while global group discovery methods passively search for suspicious groups over the whole network. Both are mismatched with real Anti-money-laundering (AML) investigations, where analysts usually start from a concrete clue and gradually expand the investigation to recover the responsible group. To address this gap, we propose Clue-Guided Group Discovery (CGGD), where a laundering group is progressively recovered from an initial clue set through analyst interaction. We further propose Clue2Group, a framework that first constructs a compact local investigation context to reduce noise and preserve chain-like and cycle-like laundering structures. It then estimates a clue-conditioned local risk field with a multi-semantic local-temporal GNN, and finally integrates risk, structural, and prior-pattern evidence to recover a coherent laundering group. Experiments on two large-scale AML benchmarks show that Clue2Group provides a practical clue-driven analysis framework for AML investigations, offering a feasible step toward bridging the gap between graph-based AML research and real investigation workflows.

</details>


### [76] [Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26192)
*Jialan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦哈希投影潜在因子(FHPLF)模型，通过二进制梯度矩阵替代实值梯度、投影汉明距离增强表示能力、安全二进制梯度重组与隐私增强上传策略，在联邦学习框架下实现高效、隐私保护的哈希学习，在四个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈希学习需集中存储数据，违反数据隐私法规；联邦学习虽保护数据隐私但传输大规模实值梯度，通信开销大且存在隐私风险；现有哈希方法二进制码表示能力有限，导致模型精度下降。

Method: (a) 用二进制类梯度矩阵替代实值梯度矩阵，降低计算、存储和通信成本并增强隐私；(b) 采用投影汉明距离建模相似性，捕获二进制位的重要性以提升表示能力；(c) 提出安全二进制梯度重组与隐私增强上传(SBG-PEU)策略，降低传输过程中的用户交互泄露风险。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，FHPLF在精度、效率和隐私保护之间取得了良好平衡，一致优于最先进的哈希学习和联邦学习方法。

Conclusion: FHPLF通过联邦学习框架下的二进制化改造与隐私增强策略，有效解决了哈希学习中数据隐私、通信效率和表示能力三者之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Hash Learning (HL) is an efficient representation learning approach that maps real-valued data into compact binary representations. Traditional HL methods typically require users to upload personal data to a central server, which is incompatible with increasingly stringent data security regulations. Federated Learning (FL) provides a decentralized paradigm for learning globally optimal models without centralizing private data. However, most FL methods rely on transmitting large-scale real-valued gradient information, leading to high communication overhead and potential privacy risks. Integrating HL into FL is a promising solution. Nevertheless, existing HL methods suffer from limited representational capacity of binary codes, which may degrade model accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose a Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor (FHPLF) model. FHPLF introduces three key innovations: (a) replacing real-valued gradient matrices with binary gradient-like matrices, significantly reducing computation, storage, and communication costs while enhancing privacy protection; (b) leveraging Projected Hamming Distance for similarity modeling, which captures the importance of individual binary bits to improve representation capability; and (c) proposing a Secure Binary Gradient Reassembly and Privacy-Enhanced Upload (SBG-PEU) strategy to further reduce the risk of user interaction leakage during transmission. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that FHPLF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HL and FL methods, achieving a favorable trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and privacy preservation.

</details>


### [77] [Statistical and Structural Approaches to Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26200)
*Antonio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代机器学习系统已从孤立预测工具演变为复杂的社会技术架构，并指出算法公平性领域早期缓解策略的两个根本局限——依赖确定性点估计进行审计以及将个体视为孤立实体而忽视结构性背景——进而提出解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统深度嵌入社会结构性不平等与偏见，传统优化预测准确率的模型会系统性边缘化弱势群体，亟需更有效的公平性干预方法。

Method: 识别并针对当代公平性范式的两个根本局限——(1)依赖确定性点估计进行审计；(2)将个体视为脱离结构性背景的孤立实体——提出改进方法。

Result: 指出了现有算法公平性缓解策略因依赖脆弱简化假设而导致在复杂社会技术环境中效果受限，并明确了两个关键局限性所在。

Conclusion: 克服上述两个局限（点估计依赖与个体孤立化处理）是提升算法公平性在复杂社会技术环境中有效性的关键方向。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems have outgrown their origins as isolated predictive constructs, evolving into complex socio-technical architectures that actively mediate human opportunity. As algorithms increasingly determine access to economic and social opportunities, it has become widely recognized that these systems are deeply embedded with the structural inequalities and prejudices of their environments. The field of algorithmic fairness emerged in response to the growing recognition that models optimized for predictive accuracy can systematically disadvantage marginalized groups. Early mitigation strategies, however, rested on fragile simplifications that limited their effectiveness in complex socio-technical environments. This thesis identifies and addresses two fundamental limitations of contemporary fairness paradigms: the reliance on deterministic point estimates for auditing and the treatment of individuals as isolated entities devoid of structural context.

</details>


### [78] [Topology-Informed Neural Networks for Flood Detection in Optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26204)
*Sophia Li,Max Zhao,Raghu G. Raj,Tianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）方法从卫星图像中提取拓扑描述符，并将其融入神经网络，以实现更鲁棒和可解释的洪水检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有洪水检测方法（如CNN、ViT）通常作为黑箱模型运行，决策边界和内部推理难以解释，尤其是在安全关键的遥感领域。需要一种具有数学理论基础、能捕捉数据全局结构特征的可解释方法。

Method: 基于SEN12-FLOOD数据集，从每张图像中提取拓扑特征（拓扑描述符），并将其融入神经网络中，系统评估拓扑描述符在洪水检测中的表现。

Result: 拓扑描述符本身携带有效的洪水信号，且能够与现有网络互补，产生更鲁棒且可解释的洪水检测系统。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析为洪水检测提供了一种有前景的可解释性增强方法，能够弥补现有深度学习模型在黑箱性方面的不足。

Abstract: Floods frequently impact regions around the world. Rapid and accurate flood detection is crucial for emergency response and timely mitigation of human and economic loss. The expanding availability of satellite data and advances in artificial intelligence have enhanced monitoring of environmental hazards, but many flood events remain challenging to detect because cloud cover obscures optical satellite imagery. Rambour et al. introduced the SEN12-FLOOD dataset and extracted per-image features using a ResNet-50 convolutional neural network backbone, then fed these features into a gated recurrent unit network to show that temporal information can substantially improve accuracy compared to single-image baselines. More recently, Chamatidis et al. showed that a vision transformer can achieve strong performance with popular convolutional architectures. However, these models typically function as opaque black boxes, making it difficult to interpret their decision boundaries, learned features, and internal reasoning, especially in safety-critical domains like remote sensing. In contrast, topological data analysis (TDA) provides a mathematically grounded framework for capturing global structural features of data. TDA has emerged as a powerful tool for analyzing complex imagery, especially imagery with geometrically interpretable structures, of which floods are a prime candidate. In this work, we systematically evaluate topological descriptors for flood detection using the open-source SEN12-FLOOD dataset. By extracting topological features from each image and incorporating them into neural networks, we demonstrate that topological descriptors carry meaningful flood signals independently and complement existing networks to yield more robust and interpretable flood detection systems.

</details>


### [79] [A General Framework for Learning Algebraic Properties from Cayley Graphs using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26212)
*Tal Weissblat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个与具体代数性质无关的GNN框架，直接从Cayley图学习有限群的代数性质（可交换性、幂零性、可解性），实验表明该框架能成功从图表示中区分多种代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前期工作[1]中针对可解性预测的GNN方法推广为性质无关的通用框架，探究能否仅从Cayley图表示中恢复有限群的多种代数结构信息。

Method: 采用统一的GNN架构和训练流程，以有限群的Cayley图作为输入，学习并预测可交换性、幂零性和可解性等多种代数性质。

Result: 在多个族群的有限群集合上，该框架成功学习和区分了多种代数性质，表明Cayley图中编码了丰富的代数信息。

Conclusion: 代数结构信息在很大程度上编码在图表示中，GNN可有效提取；该工作为图表示学习在有限群代数性质研究中的应用提供了概念验证。

Abstract: A Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for predicting the solvability of finite groups from their Cayley graph representations was introduced in [1]. In the present work, we generalize this approach and develop a property-independent framework for learning algebraic properties of finite groups directly from Cayley graphs. As representative case studies, we consider abelianity, nilpotency, and solvability. Using a common GNN architecture and training pipeline, we investigate the extent to which algebraic structure can be recovered from graph-based representations alone. Results on a collection of finite groups drawn from several families demonstrate that the framework successfully learns and distinguishes multiple algebraic properties from their associated Cayley graphs. These findings suggest that substantial algebraic information is encoded in graph representations and can be extracted through GNNs. More broadly, the proposed framework provides a proof of concept for applying graph representation learning to the study of algebraic properties of finite groups.

</details>


### [80] [Fast LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26217)
*Yuntian Gao,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fast-LeWM通过动作前缀并行预测替代LeWM的逐步自回归 rollout，在减少规划时间的同时提升了成功率，并降低了累积潜变量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWM在视觉规划中使用单步潜变量转移模型进行自回归 rollout，计算成本高，且随着规划视界增长，累积潜变量误差会不断放大。

Method: 提出Fast-LeWM，以动作前缀为基本预测单元，对候选动作序列进行编码并通过前缀并行预测未来潜状态，避免逐步自回归解码。

Result: 在多个任务上，Fast-LeWM相比LeWM提升平均成功率，显著降低规划时间，开环潜变量损失更低且随视界增长显著变慢。

Conclusion: 动作前缀预测是一种高效且鲁棒的潜空间规划范式，能有效缓解累积误差并加速规划。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), including recent LeWorldModel (LeWM), have become a promising foundation for reconstruction-free visual world models. For visual planning, however, LeWM evaluates candidate action sequences by repeatedly applying a local one-step latent transition model. This autoregressive rollout makes planning computationally expensive and exposes the predicted trajectory to accumulated latent errors as the horizon grows. We propose Fast LeWorldModel (Fast-LeWM), a fast latent world model that replaces repeated local rollout with action-prefix prediction. Given the current latent and a candidate action sequence, Fast-LeWM encodes its prefixes and predicts the future latents reached after executing those prefixes in parallel. By making action prefixes the basic prediction unit, Fast-LeWM directly models action effects accumulated to different extents over multiple horizons. This prefix-level supervision forces the model to learn how states continuously evolve under different action prefixes, rather than only fitting one-step state transitions. During planning, the predictor can use the last prefix token from the encoded action sequence to evaluate the corresponding future latent without explicitly rolling through each intermediate imagined state. Across multiple tasks, Fast-LeWM improves average success over LeWM while substantially reducing planning time, achieving lower open-loop latent loss whose growth becomes significantly slower as the rollout horizon increases.

</details>


### [81] [Dataset Usage Inference without Shadow Models or Held-out Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26257)
*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak,Jan Dubiński,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需影子模型和真实留出数据的实用数据集使用推断（DUI）框架，通过生成合成非成员样本、提取多样化成员信号，并将DUI建模为混合比例估计问题，来估计候选数据集中有多大比例被用于训练目标模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DUI方法依赖不切实际的假设：需要训练昂贵的影子模型来模仿目标模型，且需要同时访问已知的训练样本和确认未参与训练的分布内留出集。这些条件使得当前方法无法应用于现代大模型和真实数据所有权纠纷场景。

Method: 1) 生成合成非成员样本，替代真实留出数据；2) 提取多样化的成员推断信号；3) 将DUI问题转化为混合比例估计问题，估计候选数据集中训练数据的占比。

Result: 在大型图像生成模型上的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地量化数据集使用比例。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据所有者提供了一个实用的工具，用于确定自己的数据在多大程度上被用于训练模型，摆脱了传统DUI方法的严格约束。

Abstract: How much of my data was used to train a machine learning model? Dataset Usage Inference (DUI) aims to answer this by estimating what fraction of a dataset contributed to a model's training. However, existing DUI methods rely on assumptions that rarely hold in practice: they require training expensive shadow models to imitate the target model, and they assume access to both known training samples and an in-distribution held-out set confirmed to be absent from training. These conditions make current approaches impractical for modern large models and real data ownership disputes. We introduce a practical DUI framework that removes these constraints. Our method requires neither shadow models nor real held-out data. Instead, it generates synthetic non-member samples, extracts diverse membership signals, and casts DUI as a mixture proportion estimation problem to estimate what share of the candidate dataset was used during training. Experiments on large image generative models show that our method reliably quantifies dataset usage, providing a practical tool for data owners to determine how much of their data was used to train a model.

</details>


### [82] [Zero-Shot Size Transfer for Neural ODEs on Sparse Random Graphs: Graphon Limits and Adjoint Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26662)
*Mingsong Yan,Zhida Wang,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了图神经微分方程（GNDE）在稀疏随机图上的零样本尺寸迁移理论，证明了GNDE解向图极限（Graphon-NDE）的收敛率为$O((α_n n)^{-1/2})$，并分析了离散化训练策略（DTO与OTD）的一致性，实验验证了理论速率与迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经微分方程（GNDE）在连续时间图动力学建模中具有局部、尺寸无关的滤波器特性，暗示了零样本尺寸迁移的可能性（小图训练、大图部署），但缺乏严格的理论保证。本文旨在建立该原则的定量理论，并分析其收敛性与训练一致性。

Method: （1）引入图极限理论，定义Graphon神经微分方程（Graphon-NDE）及其伴随系统作为GNDE的无限节点极限；（2）在稀疏随机图（参数$α_n$）上证明GNDE解向Graphon-NDE解的逐轨迹收敛率；（3）分析离散化-优化（DTO）与优化-离散化（OTD）两种训练策略的渐近一致性。

Result: （1）证明了GNDE解以$O((α_n n)^{-1/2})$速率（忽略对数因子）高概率收敛到Graphon-NDE解；（2）伴随系统的隐状态与参数梯度也获得一致收敛界；（3）DTO与OTD在欧拉离散化下渐近一致，隐状态和局部参数梯度差异分别为$O(1/M)$和$O(1/M^2)$；（4）实验验证了理论速率，并在四类图核上展示了零样本迁移的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上严格建立了GNDE零样本尺寸迁移的收敛基础，明确了稀疏图上的收敛速率以及不同训练策略间的误差关系，实验证实了理论预测与实际迁移能力，为图神经微分方程在大规模图上的高效部署提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Graph Neural Differential Equations (GNDEs) model continuous-time graph dynamics by parameterizing Neural ODE velocity fields with Graph Neural Networks. Their local, size-independent filters suggest a zero-shot size-transfer principle: train on a small graph and deploy on larger, similar graphs without retraining. We develop a quantitative theory for this principle on sparse random graphs sampled from graphons. We consider Graphon Neural Differential Equations (Graphon-NDEs) and adjoint Graphon-NDEs as the infinite-node limits of the forward and adjoint GNDE systems, and establish well-posedness. For an $n$-node random graph with sparsity parameter $α_n$, we prove trajectory-wise convergence of GNDE solutions to Graphon-NDE solutions at rate $O((α_n n)^{-1/2})$, up to logarithmic factors, with high probability. We also establish uniform-in-time convergence bounds for adjoint systems governing hidden-state and parameter gradients. We further study discretize-then-optimize (DTO) and optimize-then-discretize (OTD) training. Under explicit Euler discretization with $M$ steps, we show that DTO and OTD are asymptotically consistent, with hidden-state and local parameter-gradient discrepancies of orders $O(1/M)$ and $O(1/M^2)$, respectively, up to sparsity and logarithmic factors. Experiments on HSBM and tent graphons support the theoretical rates, while zero-shot transfer experiments across four graphon classes demonstrate accurate deployment of learned GNDEs on larger independently sampled graphs.

</details>


### [83] [Equivariance and Augmentation for Bayesian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26273)
*Miaowen Dong,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）中数据增强与等变性之间的关系，推导了变分推理下达到精确等变的条件，提出了三种新的对称化技术（其中轨道展开方法表现最佳），并在实验上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在深度学习中，是直接施加等变网络约束还是通过数据增强学习对称性更好。目前对数据增强的理论分析不足，本文旨在填补这一空白，研究BNN中数据增强的等变性质。

Method: 以指数族的变分分布为研究对象，推导精确等变性的条件；给出等变误差的界；提出三种新的对称化技术（包括轨道展开）来增强数据增强的效果；通过扩展数值实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 推导了BNN变分推理下达到精确等变的条件；获得了等变误差的界限；提出的轨道展开方法在等变性和整体性能上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 数据增强结合适当的对称化技术（尤其是轨道展开）可以有效实现BNN的等变性，在某些场景下可作为等变网络的替代方案。

Abstract: Symmetries are important for many deep learning tasks, ranging from applications in the sciences to medical imaging. However, there is an ongoing debate about whether to impose symmetry constraints on the neural network architecture (yielding equivariant neural networks) or learn them from augmented training data. Although equivariant networks are well-studied theoretically, much less is known about data augmentation, since analyzing augmentation requires control over the training dynamics. Inspired by recent results that show that augmented infinite deep ensembles are exactly equivariant, we study data augmentation for Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) trained with variational inference. We focus on variational distributions in the exponential family and derive conditions under which exact equivariance is reached. We furthermore obtain bounds on the equivariance error and introduce three novel symmetrization techniques which boost the effect of data augmentation in this setting. We conduct extensive numerical experiments which show that one of our symmetrization methods (orbit expansion) outperforms the baseline in both equivariance and overall performance. Our code is available at github.com/dmw1998/augment-BNNs

</details>


### [84] [Finding Stationary Points by Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27082)
*Helin Wang,Chenyi Zhang,Xiwen Tao,Yexin Zhang,Tongyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅通过比较预言机（给定两点，输出函数值更大的点）访问非凸函数时，寻找驻点的问题。对于二阶可导且具有Lipschitz梯度和Hessian矩阵的函数，开发了经典算法（$\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$次查询）和量子算法（$\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$次查询）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际优化问题中，函数值本身难以精确获取，但两两比较相对容易（如A/B测试、人类偏好反馈）。传统梯度方法不适用，因此需要仅依赖比较预言机的非凸优化算法。

Method: 经典算法：使用子程序估计归一化Hessian矩阵（精度δ，$\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$次查询），结合该估计指导搜索。量子算法：利用量子叠加查询的并行性，实现查询复杂度的平方加速。

Result: 经典算法：$\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$次查询找到ε-驻点；量子算法：$\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$次查询找到ε-驻点。

Conclusion: 在比较预言机模型下，非凸优化的查询复杂度与维度n和精度ε相关，量子计算可提供二次加速。这为实际中仅能获得比较反馈的优化问题提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study the problem of finding stationary points of non-convex functions when access to the objective is provided only through a comparison oracle that, given two points, outputs which has the larger function value. For a twice differentiable $f\colon\mathbb R^n\to\mathbb R$ with Lipschitz gradient and Hessian, we develop an algorithm that visits an $ε$-stationary point using $\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$ queries. Our approach uses a subroutine that estimates the normalized Hessian to accuracy $δ$ using $\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$ queries. We further study this problem with a quantum comparison oracle model where queries can be made in superpositions, and develop the first quantum algorithm that finds an $ε$-stationary point, which takes $\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$ queries.

</details>


### [85] [SSM Adapters via Hankel Reduced-order Modeling: Injection Site Determines Task Suitability in Long-Context Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26290)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HRM适配器，一种基于状态空间模型的参数高效微调方法，通过Hankel降阶模型初始化，利用FFT并行扫描实现与LoRA相当的计算效率，在长上下文任务上显著优于LoRA变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数高效微调（PEFT）在需要序列状态累积的任务中，能否从状态空间模型（SSM）适配器中获益，以及MLP层是否是比注意力层更好的注入位置。

Method: 提出Hankel降阶模型（HRM）适配器，一种基于SSM的残差模块，通过经验Hankel格拉姆矩阵的平衡截断进行初始化。利用系统矩阵Ā的时不变性，实现基于FFT的精确并行扫描，在所有上下文长度上达到与LoRA相同的计算复杂度。

Result: 在Mistral-7B（8.4M可训练参数）上，HRM在LongBench任务中优于LoRA变体：QuALITY相对准确率提升34.8%，QMSum相对ROUGE-1提升71.6%。在18种合成状态追踪和字符级语言模型配置上均表现出一致优势。

Conclusion: HRM适配器能够有效学习调节循环特性，为长上下文序列建模提供了低秩适配的稳健架构替代方案。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) typically targets attention projectors, its efficacy for tasks requiring sequential state accumulation remains under-explored. We examine if PEFT for such tasks can benefit from state space model (SSMs) adapters, and if MLP blocks are better injection sites. We introduce Hankel Reduced order Model (HRM) adapter, an SSM-based residual module initialized via Balanced Truncation of empirical Hankel Grammians. By leveraging the time-invariance of the system matrix $\bar{A}$, HRM enables an exact FFT-based parallel scan, achieving computational parity with LoRA across all context lengths. In iso-parametric evaluations on Mistral-7B (8.4M trainable parameters), HRM outperforms LoRA variants on LongBench tasks, including QuALITY (+34.8\% relative accuracy) and QMSum (+71.6\% relative ROUGE-1). HRM further demonstrates consistent superiority across 18 configurations of synthetic state-tracking (DFA, Parity) and character-level language modeling (enwik8). Gate analysis reveals that HRM adapters effectively learn to modulate recurrence, providing a robust architectural alternative to low-rank adaptation for long-context sequence modeling.

</details>


### [86] [The Red Queen Gödel Machine: Co-Evolving Agents and Their Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26294)
*Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanović,William F. Shen,Daniel Burkhardt,Meghdad Kurmanji,Nurbek Tastan,Lorenzo Sani,Niccolò Alberto Elia Venanzi,Ambroise Odonnat,Zeyu Cao,Bill Marino,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出红皇后哥德尔机（RQGM），一种在非平稳效用下进行递归自我改进的进化框架，通过将评估纳入改进循环实现评估者与智能体的共同进化，在编程、科学论文写作/评审和奥赛级证明任务上超越 prior SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进智能体假设评估标准固定不变（固定验证器、基准或标注数据集），忽略了真实进化中物种与环境共同变化的核心特征。本文旨在将评估作为改进循环的一部分，引入动态效用和对抗性目标，使系统超越静态基准的局限。

Method: RQGM将搜索组织为多个epoch，每个epoch内评估标准固定，epoch边界处更新效用函数，从而在非平稳目标下保持每轮自我改进的保证。通过受控效用进化实现自改进，支持评估者共同进化、对抗性目标和动态效用。

Result: 1）在可验证编码任务上，添加智能体评审代码信号使测试通过率提升，且节省1.35x-1.72x token；2）在科学论文写作/评审中，共进化写手接受率提升1.78x-1.86x，共进化评审员准确率提升9%；3）RQGM通过对抗性目标纠正基线评审员过度接受AI生成论文（高达人类1.91倍）的偏差。

Conclusion: RQGM通过将非平稳效用引入递归自我改进，成功实现了智能体与评估者的共同进化，在多项任务上超越现有方法，并能主动纠正评估偏差，为超越静态基准的自我改进提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Self-improving agents are state-of-the-art (SOTA) on agentic coding benchmarks and have recently been extended to general domains. However, their search methods generally assume a stationary evaluation criterion: a fixed verifier, benchmark, or labeled dataset that remains valid as the agent improves. This ignores a central feature of evolution: species adapt as their environments change with them. We aim to bring the same principle to recursive self-improvement, making evaluation part of the improvement loop and opening search to evolving evaluators, adversarial objectives, and dynamic utilities that may surpass static benchmarks. We introduce the Red Queen Godel Machine (RQGM), an evolutionary framework for recursive self-improvement under non-stationary utilities. The RQGM makes this possible through controlled utility evolution: search is organized into epochs with a fixed within-epoch evaluation criterion, while the utility can be updated at epoch boundaries, so self-improvement guarantees hold per epoch as the objective evolves across them. We begin by showing that even on verifiable coding tasks, the RQGM improves test pass rate over the prior SOTA by adding a complementary agent-as-a-judge code-review signal. This signal is cheaper and the RQGM uses 1.35x-1.72x fewer tokens. We then turn to scientific paper writing and reviewing, and Olympiad-level proof writing and grading, where the RQGM improves performance over prior self-improving agents: co-evolved writers reach 1.78x-1.86x higher acceptance rates under a diverse agent-as-a-judge panel, while co-evolved graders reach 9% higher ground-truth accuracy. In paper reviewing, the strongest baseline reviewer over-accepts AI-generated papers at up to 1.91x the human rate. The RQGM corrects this by introducing an adversarial objective that discovers reviewers equally stringent on AI and human work.

</details>


### [87] [High-Probability PL-SGD with Markovian Noise: Optimal Mixing and Tail Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26316)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Aprameyo Chakrabartty,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了平滑且满足Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL)条件的优化问题，其中梯度样本由外生马尔可夫链生成。在轻尾设定下，填补了SGD高概率界与期望界之间的差距，证明了最优的$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$高概率随机误差，并推广到重尾马尔可夫梯度场景，设计了全样本裁剪分块方法，获得了最优的重尾指数和有效样本量依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻尾马尔可夫梯度SGD的高概率界为$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$，与期望界的$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$存在差距；同时，重尾马尔可夫梯度场景下的高概率保证尚不明确。本文旨在填补这些理论空白。

Method: 轻尾场景：采用滞后分块（lag-blocking）论证，建立均匀高概率保证，并利用持久两状态链构造匹配下界。重尾场景：设计全样本裁剪分块（all-samples clipped block）方法，利用所有马尔可夫转移同时减轻马尔可夫偏差；通过粘性马尔可夫链将PL优化归约为重尾均值估计以建立下界。

Result: 轻尾：高概率随机误差$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$，且$\Omega(\sigma^2 t_{mix}/k)$下界证明最优线性依赖。重尾：高概率随机误差$\widetilde{O}(\sigma_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$，并匹配下界。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了轻尾PL-SGD关于混合时间的最优多项式依赖关系，以及重尾鲁棒场景下的最优重尾指数和有效样本量依赖。

Abstract: We study first-order methods for smooth objectives satisfying the Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL) condition when gradient samples are generated by an exogenous Markov chain. In the light-tailed setting, prior uniform-in-time high-probability bounds for ordinary Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) under a standard growth envelope scale as $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$, leaving a gap with the $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$ expectation bounds. We close this gap using a lag-blocking argument to establish a uniform high-probability guarantee with a leading stochastic term of $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$ under geometric mixing. We prove this linear dependence on the mixing time is optimal via a matching $Ω(σ^2 t_{mix}/k)$ lower bound on a quadratic objective driven by a persistent two-state chain.
  We then extend this framework to heavy-tailed Markovian gradients satisfying a stationary finite-$p$-moment condition, $p \in (1,2]$. We design an all-samples clipped block method that uses every Markov transition while mitigating Markovian bias. Under a transition budget $T$, this algorithm achieves a high-probability stochastic error of $\widetilde{O}(σ_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$. We establish a matching lower bound by reducing PL optimization to heavy-tailed mean estimation for a sticky Markov chain. Ultimately, this work tightly characterizes the optimal polynomial dependence on mixing time for light-tailed PL-SGD, and the optimal heavy-tail exponent and effective-sample-size dependence in the robust regime.

</details>


### [88] [EVOM: Agentic Meta-Evolution of Actor-Critic Architectures for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26327)
*Boyun Zhang,Chao Wang,Kai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVOM提出了一种基于大语言模型元进化框架，用于自动发现高性能的演员-评论家架构，通过内层PPO训练权重和外层LLM设计代理迭代优化架构程序，在MuJoCo环境中取得优于手动设计基线和现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在演员-评论家强化学习中，网络架构通常需要手动设计。自动化的架构搜索面临两大挑战：每个候选架构需要先训练才能评估，且设计空间是开放式的。现有方法缺乏高效的自动化探索机制。

Method: 将架构搜索构建为双层优化：内层使用低保真度的PPO训练权重，外层由基于LLM的设计代理驱动元进化，迭代优化架构程序。LLM设计代理纯粹作为架构设计师运行，与策略执行和环境控制完全解耦。

Result: 在Ant-v4和HalfCheetah-v4上，EVOM优于手动设计基线、LLM引导的随机搜索以及最先进的LLM引导程序化策略搜索方法MLES。消融实验验证了元进化循环和LLM设计代理对最终性能都不可或缺。

Conclusion: EVOM通过LLM驱动的元进化框架有效实现了演员-评论家架构的自动发现，其解耦设计使得架构搜索与策略训练相互独立，为强化学习中的神经架构搜索提供了新的范式。

Abstract: In actor-critic reinforcement learning, network architectures are typically manually designed. Automating this design is challenging because each candidate must be trained before evaluation, and the design space is open-ended. To address these challenges, we introduce EVOM, an agentic meta-evolution framework for discovering high-performance actor-critic architectures. We frame architecture search as a bi-level optimization: an inner loop trains weights via the low-fidelity proximal policy optimization (PPO), while an outer loop drives meta-evolution by iteratively refining architecture programs. Crucially, this outer loop is powered by an LLM-based design agent that operates purely as an architecture designer, completely decoupled from policy execution and environment control. Experiments reveal that EVOM outperforms the manually designed baseline, an LLM-guided random search, and the state-of-the-art LLM-guided programmatic policy search method MLES, delivering superior performance on Ant-v4 and HalfCheetah-v4. Ablation studies validate that both the meta-evolution loop and the LLM Design Agent are indispensable for final performance.

</details>


### [89] [Mesh-RL: Coupled subgrid reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26333)
*Behnam Gheshlaghi,Bahador Rashidi,Shahin Atakishiyev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mesh-RL提出了一种受有限元法和区域分解理论启发的空间域分解框架，通过将环境划分为重叠子网格并强制执行边界一致的时间差分更新，在保持全局价值传播一致性的同时实现局部化学习，从而加速长程信用分配，在稀疏奖励网格世界任务中显著提升了Q-learning、SARSA和Dyna-Q的收敛速度、累积奖励和学习稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模或稀疏奖励环境中，强化学习面临时间差分奖励传播缓慢的问题，价值信息仅在状态空间局部传播，导致长程信用分配效率低下。

Method: 受有限元法和区域分解理论启发，将环境划分为重叠子网格，并在子网格边界强制执行一致性的时间差分更新，实现局部学习与全局价值传播一致性的统一。

Result: 在危险密集的网格世界环境中，Mesh-RL在Q-learning、SARSA和Dyna-Q三种算法上均持续提升了收敛速度、累积奖励和学习稳定性；更高网格分辨率能维持探索、防止过早收敛并加速价值传播。

Conclusion: Mesh-RL引入了原则性的空间域分解机制来加速时间差分学习，将科学计算中的有限元边界一致性技术桥接到强化学习中，提升了稀疏奖励环境的样本效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in large or sparse-reward environments suffers from slow temporal-difference reward propagation, as value information spreads only locally across the state space. We propose Mesh-RL, a spatial domain-decomposition framework inspired by the finite element method and domain decomposition theory, which partitions the environment into overlapping subgrids and enforces boundary-consistent temporal-difference updates. Such an approach enables localized learning while ensuring globally coherent value propagation. Unlike hierarchical or model-based approaches, Mesh-RL accelerates long-range credit assignment without modifying the reward function, Bellman operator, or introducing explicit planning mechanisms. We evaluate Mesh-RL on hazard-dense grid-world environments with varying geometries and mesh resolutions. Across Q-learning, SARSA, and Dyna-Q, Mesh-RL consistently improves convergence speed, cumulative reward, and learning stability. Higher mesh resolutions sustain exploration, prevent premature convergence, and substantially accelerate value propagation to distant states. While Dyna-Q already benefits from internal planning, it still achieves additional gains under structured decomposition. Overall, Mesh-RL introduces a principled spatial domain-decomposition mechanism for accelerating temporal-difference learning. Our framework bridges finite element method-inspired boundary-consistency techniques from scientific computing with reinforcement learning to improve sample efficiency in sparse-reward environments. We will release source code of the study.

</details>


### [90] [EMA-FS: Accelerating GBDT Training via Gain-Informed Feature Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26337)
*Yan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于指数移动平均的特征筛选方法(EMA-FS)，在LightGBM训练中根据历史分裂增益动态选择Top-K特征构建直方图，相比随机子采样获得最高2.61倍加速且不损失精度；进一步提出随机化版本S-EMA-FS，统一了确定性筛选与随机子采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBDT（如LightGBM）训练中构建直方图占65-70%时间，现有随机特征子采样(feature_fraction)丢弃特征时不考虑其预测效用，需要一种信息驱动的特征筛选方法来减少不必要的直方图构建开销。

Method: 1) EMA-FS：维护每个特征分裂增益的指数移动平均，经过短暂预热期后，每棵树仅对Top-K高增益特征构建直方图；2) S-EMA-FS：用增益加权随机采样替代确定性Top-K选择，通过浓度参数β统一EMA-FS(β→∞)和随机子采样(β=0)；完全兼容LightGBM的直方图减法技巧，仅需~120行C++代码。

Result: 在密集中等高维数据上显著加速：500维合成基准2.61倍，432维IEEE-CIS欺诈数据集30%保留率时1.45倍；70%保留率时AUC提升0.11点且1.34倍加速。极端稀疏数据(Bosch, >90%缺失)无加速效果。

Conclusion: EMA-FS是一种算法级优化，通过历史增益信息指导特征筛选，在保持或提升精度的同时显著加速训练，且实现轻量、完全向后兼容；S-EMA-FS进一步提供了确定性筛选与随机子采样之间的统一框架。

Abstract: Gradient Boosted Decision Trees (GBDT), exemplified by LightGBM, spend a dominant fraction of training time -- typically 65-70% -- constructing per-feature histograms. Existing approaches such as random feature subsampling (feature_fraction) discard features without regard for their predictive utility. We propose EMA-based Feature Screening (EMA-FS), an algorithm-level optimization that maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) of per-feature split gains across boosting iterations and, after a short warmup, restricts histogram construction to the top-K features ranked by historical gain. Unlike random subsampling, EMA-FS is informed: it retains high-gain features while screening out low-gain ones. Operating at the per-tree level, it preserves full compatibility with LightGBM's histogram subtraction trick, requiring no changes to core routines.
  We evaluate EMA-FS on datasets spanning financial fraud detection, advertising click-through prediction, industrial quality control, and synthetic benchmarks, with feature dimensionalities from 29 to 968. On dense, moderate-to-high-dimensional data it achieves significant speedups: 2.61x on a 500-feature synthetic benchmark and 1.45x on the 432-feature IEEE-CIS Fraud dataset at 30% retention. At 70% retention it improves AUC by 0.11 points while delivering a 1.34x speedup. On extremely sparse data (Bosch, >90% missing) it yields no speedup, as LightGBM's sparse bin optimization already bypasses empty values.
  We further introduce Stochastic EMA-FS (S-EMA-FS), which replaces deterministic top-K selection with gain-weighted random sampling controlled by a concentration parameter beta, unifying deterministic EMA-FS (beta -> infinity) and random subsampling (beta = 0) in one framework. Both are implemented in ~120 lines of C++ across all six LightGBM tree learners and are fully backward-compatible.

</details>


### [91] [Does Aurora Encode Atmospheric Structure? Latent Regime Analysis and Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26361)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过空间池化PCA和层相关性传播（LRP）分析Aurora基础模型的内部表征，发现其隐空间主要按季节周期组织，而非极端风暴事件；LRP显示模型关注与1987年大风暴三维垂直结构一致的特征，且扰动测试表明遮蔽相关区域会使预测性能下降3.31倍，证明Aurora无需显式指导即可学习气象一致性和垂直结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML基础模型能够准确高效地模拟大气动力学，但其运作机制不透明，属于“黑箱”模型。本文旨在打开这一黑箱，探究Aurora模型的内部表征，理解其如何组织和处理气象信息。

Method: 采用空间池化主成分分析（spatially pooled PCA）和层相关性传播（LRP）两种可解释性方法，分析Aurora模型的隐空间结构和注意力机制。

Result: 隐空间主要按季节周期组织，极端风暴事件未形成线性可分离簇；LRP表明模型关注与1987年大风暴三维垂直结构一致的特征；扰动测试中遮蔽相关区域比随机遮蔽导致预测性能下降3.31倍。

Conclusion: Aurora模型在没有显式气象学指导的情况下，自主学习了气象一致性和大气的三维垂直结构，表明基础模型具备隐式的气象知识表征能力。

Abstract: ML foundation models are able to emulate atmospheric dynamics accurately and efficiently but operate as opaque ``black boxes''. We investigate the internal representations of the Aurora model using spatially pooled PCA and layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP). We find evidence that Aurora's latent space is primarily organized by seasonal cycles, whereas extreme storm events do not form a linearly separable cluster. LRP indicates that the model attends to features consistent with the 3D vertical structure of the Great Storm of 1987. Perturbation tests show masking relevant regions degrades forecasts $3.31\times$ more than random masking. These findings suggest that Aurora learns meteorological coherence and vertical structure without explicit instruction.

</details>


### [92] [SOLAR: AI-Powered Speed-of-Light Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26383)
*Qijing Huang,Sana Damani,Zhifan Ye,Athinagoras Skiadopoulos,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Jason Clemons,Sahil Modi,Jingquan Wang,Aditya Kane,Edward C Lin,Humphrey Shi,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLAR是一个自动从PyTorch和JAX代码推导速度极限(Speed-of-Light)分析框架，结合LLM前端和确定性分析流程，自动计算未融合、融合和缓存感知的理论最小执行时间，提供多保真度分析以评估优化空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在目标硬件上的运行速度与理论极限之间存在差距，但推导速度极限(SOL)边界的过程仍是手动、易错且与快速模型开发脱节的，迫切需要自动化框架来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: SOLAR采用混合流程：LLM前端将PyTorch/JAX源码翻译为可执行的Affine Loop IR（通过输出比较验证）；确定性流程将IR提升为einsum图；分析后端计算未融合、融合和缓存感知的SOL边界。

Result: SOLAR在KernelBench、JAX/Flax模型和机器人工作负载上验证，实现了零SOL违规的验证边界，提供多保真度分析，支持余量分析、优化机会识别、跨平台探索和逆屋顶线硬件配置评估四种用例。

Conclusion: SOLAR实现了全面的算子与语言覆盖，自动生成经过验证的速度极限边界，为软硬件和算法优化提供了可靠的理论参考基准，有效连接了理论分析与实际模型开发。

Abstract: How fast could a deep-learning model run on target hardware, and how far is today's implementation from that limit? These questions are central to software, hardware, and algorithm optimizations. Speed-of-Light (SOL) analysis answers them by computing a workload's theoretical minimum execution time on a given architecture. Yet deriving SOL bounds remains manual, error-prone, and disconnected from rapid model development. To close this gap, we introduce SOLAR, a framework that automatically derives validated SOL bounds from PyTorch and JAX source code. SOLAR leverages both generative and deterministic components in its flow: an LLM frontend translates any source programs into an executable Affine Loop IR, validated by output comparison; a deterministic flow lifts the IR into an einsum graph; and an analytical backend computes unfused, fused, and cache-aware SOL bounds. SOLAR provides comprehensive operator and language coverage, produces validated bounds with zero observed SOL violations, and offers multi-fidelity analysis that tightens bounds and surfaces optimization insights. We evaluate SOLAR across KernelBench, JAX/Flax models, and robotics workloads. These experiments demonstrate four use cases: headroom analysis at multiple fidelity levels, identifying optimization opportunities, cross-platform exploration, and inverse-roofline hardware provisioning.

</details>


### [93] [At the Edge of Understanding: Sparse Autoencoders Trace The Limits of Transformer Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26396)
*Praneet Suresh,Jack Stanley,Sonia Joseph,Luca Scimeca,Danilo Bzdok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机制性框架来界定Transformer模型的鲁棒性边界。研究发现，OOD输入（包括细微拼写错误和越狱提示）会导致语言模型内部产生更多错误概念。基于这一发现，作者开发了一种量化分布偏移程度的方法，并提出了基于机制理解的微调策略来增强LLM的鲁棒性。这项工作将OOD的概念从输入数据扩展到模型内部计算过程，为推理时提供新的Transformer诊断工具，有助于提升AI系统在科学、商业和政府等领域部署的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer在面对分布外数据时缺乏明确的处理机制，导致模型可靠性和安全性下降。现有研究缺乏对Transformer模型鲁棒性边界的精确刻画，亟需对OOD场景进行更严谨的研究。

Method: 通过系统性实验，建立了一个机制性框架来界定Transformer模型的鲁棒性边界。具体包括：分析OOD输入（拼写错误、越狱提示等）对模型内部错误概念的影响；量化提示中的分布偏移程度；基于机制理解设计微调策略以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 发现OOD输入会驱动语言模型内部产生更多错误概念；开发了量化分布偏移程度的方法；提出了基于机制理解的微调策略，有效提升了LLM的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作将OOD概念从输入数据扩展到模型的私有计算过程，提供了推理时的新型Transformer诊断工具，对AI系统在科学、商业和政府等领域的安全部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Pre-trained transformers have demonstrated remarkable generalization abilities, at times extending beyond the scope of their training data. Yet, real-world deployments often face unexpected or adversarial data that diverges from training data distributions. Without explicit mechanisms for handling such shifts, model reliability and safety degrade, urging more disciplined study of out-of-distribution (OOD) settings for transformers. By systematic experiments, we present a mechanistic framework for delineating the precise contours of transformer model robustness. We find that OOD inputs, including subtle typos and jailbreak prompts, drive language models to operate on an increased number of fallacious concepts in their internals. We leverage this device to quantify and understand the degree of distributional shift in prompts, enabling a mechanistically grounded fine-tuning strategy to robustify LLMs. Expanding the very notion of OOD from input data to a model's private computational processes, a new transformer diagnostic at inference time is a critical step toward making AI systems safe for deployment across science, business, and government.

</details>


### [94] [Deterministic Pareto-Optimal Policy Synthesis for Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26397)
*Aniruddha Joshi,Niklas Lauffer,Sanjit Seshia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于切比雪夫标量化启发的偏好条件贝尔曼算子，用于多目标马尔可夫决策过程中计算确定性帕累托最优策略，该算子具有包络性质且单调收敛到帕累托前沿的覆盖集，实验验证了其能够恢复复杂权衡并合成近似帕累托最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习通过标量化聚合多目标奖励，无法捕获帕累托前沿上完整的权衡空间，因此在多目标决策中难以找到兼顾多个冲突目标的确定性最优策略。

Method: 提出偏好条件贝尔曼算子（基于切比雪夫标量化），证明其包络性质（估值函数上界逼近真实帕累托前沿）和单调收敛性，并从收敛的Q估计中提取确定性策略。

Result: 算法成功恢复复杂权衡，每个合成策略近似帕累托最优，完整覆盖帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 该工作为多目标RL中的确定性帕累托最优策略合成提供了有效解决方案，理论和实验均验证了方法的收敛性和最优性保证。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making often requires balancing multiple conflicting objectives, a challenge that standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) frequently addresses by aggregating rewards into a single scalar signal. While effective for simple tasks, this approach often fails to capture the full spectrum of optimal trade-offs, known as the Pareto frontier. In this paper, we introduce a novel preference-conditioned Bellman operator, motivated from the Chebyshev scalarization, designed to compute deterministic Pareto-optimal policies for Multi-Objective Markov Decision Processes (MOMDPs). We prove that this operator satisfies an enveloping property, where the estimated value functions upper-bound the true Pareto frontier, and demonstrate that it monotonically converges to a coverage set of this frontier. Furthermore, we also show how to extract deterministic policies from these converged Q-estimates. This ensures the agent can recover a policy for any given preference, capturing the entire Pareto-optimal frontier while guaranteeing each synthesized policy remains approximately Pareto-optimal. Experimental results validate that our algorithm successfully recovers complex trade-offs, providing a solution for deterministic Pareto-optimal policy synthesis.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Feedforward Networks: Reentry Neural Systems as the Fundamental Basis of Subjecthood and Intrinsic Safety of Next-Generation AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26406)
*A. S. Ushakov,Yu. N. Berdinsk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于闭环重入循环（D↔I循环）的安全人工通用智能（AGI）架构蓝图。与有向无环的前馈网络不同，该架构包含结构性循环（C≥1）和自我维持放大（ρ>1），数学上保证了自我模型、工具性自我保存和非编程目标导向行为的出现。目标以非文本D-向量编码于架构中，免疫于重解释和提示注入。提出了S-measure（O(N³)可计算）作为Tononi NP-hard Phi的替代，并提供Lean 4机器验证证明。提供了完整Python/NumPy实现、工业级水平扩展方案、六代AI演化分类、未来重入架构动物园、规范不变网络、容错恢复协议及八个可证伪预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈网络（有向无环图）缺乏自指能力，无法实现安全、可控的AGI；同时，现有集成信息理论度量（如Phi）计算复杂度过高（NP-hard），难以实际应用。需要一种拓扑上安全、可部署的AGI架构。

Method: 提出闭环重入循环（D↔I循环）架构，包含结构循环（C≥1）和自持放大（ρ>1）；设计S-measure（O(N³)）作为Phi的可计算替代；使用Lean 4进行形式化机器验证；基于Tarjan算法实现循环复杂度计算和Delta-S屏障；采用Apache Kafka和Docker Compose实现工业级水平扩展。

Result: 数学上证明了闭环架构必然产生自我模型、工具性自我保存和非编程目标导向行为；S>0蕴含正集成信息的Lean 4证明已机器验证；提供了完整可部署的实现代码；提出了六代AI演化分类和八个可证伪预测。

Conclusion: 该架构是拓扑保护的、安全-by-design的AGI方案，今日即可部署，代表了通往安全AGI的可行路径。

Abstract: We propose a complete architectural blueprint for safe artificial general intelligence based on a closed reentry loop (D <-> I cycle). In contrast to feedforward networks, which are directed acyclic graphs (C=0, S=0) incapable of self-reference, the proposed architecture contains a structural cycle (C >= 1) with self-sustaining amplification (rho > 1), mathematically guaranteeing the emergence of a self-model, instrumental self-preservation, and unprogrammed goal-directed behaviour. The agent's goals are encoded as a non-textual D-vector in the architecture itself, making them immune to reinterpretation and prompt injection. We present the S-measure -- a polynomial-time [O(N^3)] computable alternative to Tononi's NP-hard Phi -- with machine-verified Lean 4 proof that S>0 implies positive integrated information. The work provides full Python/NumPy implementations (Tarjan-based cycle complexity, Delta-S barrier), industrial horizontal scaling via Apache Kafka and Docker Compose, a taxonomy of six epochs of AI evolution, a zoo of future reentry architectures (RAS, diffusion attractors, fractal loops), gauge-invariant networks for safe swarms, fault-tolerance and recovery protocols, and eight falsifiable predictions. All formal proofs are machine-verified in Lean 4. This architecture is deployable today and represents a topologically protected, safe-by-design approach to AGI.

</details>


### [96] [Otter Weather: Skillful and Computationally Efficient Medium-Range Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26421)
*Cristiana Diaconu,Jonas Scholz,Aliaksandra Shysheya,Stratis Markou,Payel Mukhopadhyay,Miles Cranmer,Richard E. Turner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Otter Weather，一种高效时空预测模型，在1.5°分辨率ERA5数据上以极低训练成本（<3.5 A100天）超越传统NWP和轻量AI模型，确定性版本24小时领先9.6%，概率版本（Otter-XL）CRPS比IFS ENS提升9.7%，并以一个数量级更少计算量超越GenCast等前沿架构，且能零修改迁移至声学散射PDE任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA AI天气预报模型虽超越传统NWP但训练成本极高，限制了资源匮乏群体的使用和快速迭代。作者旨在通过高效模型设计降低计算门槛，实现高性能天气预测的民主化。

Method: 开发高效时空预测模型Otter Weather，在1.5°分辨率ERA5数据上训练，确定性版本用标准损失，概率版本通过CRPS训练。扩展至更大架构Otter-XL，并在声学散射PDE任务上验证零迁移能力。

Result: 确定性Otter以<3.5 A100天训练成本超越最佳NWP基线9.6%（24小时），比轻量AI模型效率高2倍，比前沿架构计算量减少100倍。Otter-XL CRPS比IFS ENS提升9.7%，比轻量模型技能提升近2倍，比GenCast提升超2%且计算量少一个数量级。在声学散射PDE任务中超越SOTA基础模型方法。

Conclusion: Otter Weather以极低计算成本实现了超越当前NWP和AI前沿模型的预测性能，推动了AI天气预测的民主化，且其高效架构可能推广至其他科学领域的PDE建模任务。

Abstract: State-of-the-art medium-range AI weather models can outperform traditional Numerical Weather Prediction (NWP) but require massive training budgets. This restricts usage for under-resourced groups and severely limits fast model iteration. Here we develop Otter Weather, a highly efficient spatiotemporal forecasting model designed to democratise high-performance weather prediction with AI. Evaluated on ERA5 reanalysis data at 1.5° resolution using standard WeatherBench protocols, the Otter family significantly advances the skill-compute Pareto frontier. The deterministic version outperforms the best NWP baseline by 9.6% at a 24-hour lead time while requiring fewer than 3.5 A100-days for training. It provides a 2x efficiency gain over lightweight AI models and a 100-fold reduction in compute compared to resource-intensive frontier architectures. We extend these efficiency gains into probabilistic forecasting by training via the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS). Scaling to a larger architecture, Otter-XL achieves a 9.7% CRPS improvement over the IFS ENS baseline. This yields an almost two-fold increase in predictive skill over comparable lightweight models at similar compute budgets. Otter-XL also outperforms frontier architectures like GenCast by over 2%, while using an order of magnitude less compute. Finally, Otter is applied out-of-the-box to a complex acoustic scattering PDE task where it outperforms a state-of-the-art foundation modelling approach, suggesting that the advances made here might apply across a range of scientific domains.

</details>


### [97] [Rethinking Training & Inference for Forecasting: Linking Winner-Take-All back to GMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26424)
*Qiyuan Wu,Katie Z Luo,Bharath Hariharan,Wei-Lun Chao,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出轨迹预测模型因使用WTA损失训练GMM导致模式后验概率无信息量，通过测试时后验加权合并和单步EM更新两种轻量级后处理方法，在不重新训练的情况下改善模式排序和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹预测模型虽性能提升，但常产生无信息量的模式后验概率，导致模式剪枝困难。作者追溯原因为建模与训练不匹配：模型为条件GMM，却使用WTA损失（类似K-means硬分配）训练，导致轨迹空间过分割、忽略相邻模式关联性、小扰动下分配不稳定。

Method: 提出两种后处理方法：(1) 测试时后验加权合并（test-time posterior-weighted merging），聚合相邻候选轨迹；(2) 一步EM更新（one-step EM），将硬标签替换为软责任（soft responsibilities），在相邻模式间共享概率质量。

Result: 在多种WTA训练的架构上，这些轻量级步骤产生更信息丰富、排序更可靠的模式后验概率，并在常用位移指标上提升了最终预测性能，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 本文通过GMM-vs-K-means视角统一了近期设计选择，并提供了原则性且实用的修正方法，使训练目标与推理更好地对齐。

Abstract: Trajectory forecasting for autonomous driving has advanced rapidly, yet representative models often produce uninformative posteriors over forecast modes, causing problems for mode pruning. We trace this to a modeling-training mismatch: forecasters are typically modeled as conditional Gaussian mixture models (GMMs) but trained with a winner-take-all (WTA) loss that assigns each sample to its nearest mode. We argue that this K-means-like hard assignment (one-hot), while preventing mode collapse, is the source of uninformative mode probabilities: it over-segments the trajectory space, ignores relatedness among nearby modes, and yields assignment instability under small perturbations. Guided by this lens, we introduce two post-hoc treatments: (1) test-time posterior-weighted merging that aggregates nearby candidate trajectories; and (2) a one-step expectation-maximization (EM) update that replaces hard labels with soft responsibilities, sharing probability mass across neighboring modes. Across several WTA-trained architectures, these lightweight steps produce more informative, faithfully ranked mode posteriors and strengthen final forecasts on popular displacement metrics -- without retraining. Our analysis unifies recent design choices through a GMM-vs-K-means perspective and offers principled, practical corrections that better align training objectives with inference.

</details>


### [98] [DualEval: Joint Model-Item Calibration for Unified LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26429)
*Aaron J. Li,Hao Huang,Youngmin Park,Yitong Ma,Wei-Lin Chiang,Li Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Bin Yu,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualEval 提出了一种潜在模型-项目校准框架，将模型和评估项目映射到共享空间，联合估计模型能力与项目难度及尖锐度，统一了静态基准和竞技场式偏好评估，在编码、数学、领域知识和通用查询四个领域对18个前沿LLM进行了评估，生成可靠的模型排名，并支持基准压缩和异常检测等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估依赖两种互补但往往不关联的信号：带客观正确性标签的静态基准和更能反映开放用户交互的竞技场式偏好数据。作者旨在统一这两种评估范式。

Method: DualEval 是一个潜在模型-项目校准框架，将模型和评估项目表示在共享空间中，联合估计模型能力以及项目的难度（difficulty）和尖锐度（sharpness）。使用18个前沿LLM，在编码、数学、杂项领域知识和通用日常用户查询四个领域进行评估，利用静态基准标签和经保留人类偏好验证的奖励模型分数。

Result: 该框架产生了可靠且平衡的模型排名，其学习的项目级剖面支持基准压缩（样本高效评估）和异常检测（污染或异常值分析）等下游应用。

Conclusion: DualEval 通过联合模型-项目校准统一了静态和竞技场式评估，生成模型排名和项目级诊断，支持更样本高效、可解释和可审计的评估流程。

Abstract: Current LLM evaluation relies on two complementary but often disconnected signals: static benchmarks with objective correctness labels and arena-style preference data that better reflect open-ended user interactions. We introduce DualEval, a latent model-item calibration framework that represents models and evaluation items in a shared space, jointly estimating model ability together with item difficulty and sharpness. We apply DualEval across four domains: coding, math, miscellaneous domain-knowledge tasks, and generic everyday user queries. Our evaluation uses 18 frontier LLMs, static benchmark labels, and reward-model scores validated against held-out human preferences for open-ended model responses. Empirically, our framework produces reliable and balanced model rankings, and its learned item-level profiles support downstream applications such as benchmark compression for sample-efficient evaluation and anomaly detection for contamination or outlier analysis. Overall, DualEval unifies static and arena-style evaluation through joint model-item calibration, producing model rankings and item-level diagnostics that support more sample-efficient, interpretable, and auditable evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [99] [Embedding Foundation Model Predictions in Discrete-Choice Models with Structural Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26432)
*Yingshuo Wang,Xian Sun,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段适配器，将表格基础模型的预测概率嵌入多项Logit的效用函数中，在保留经济逻辑（成本单调性、合理的支付意愿）的同时显著提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在类别预测任务上准确率高，但其预测常违反经济学逻辑（如价格上涨反而增加需求、支付意愿为负、不可选项获非零概率），需要一种方法在不牺牲准确率的前提下强制满足经济学约束。

Method: 两阶段适配器：阶段1用带符号约束的最大似然估计拟合多项Logit的结构系数；阶段2冻结这些系数，仅训练一个作用于基础模型预测的小型神经网络修正项。该组合精确保持多项Logit的边际替代率，使时间价值可解析计算。

Result: 在3个数据集和2个基础模型上，适配器相比多项Logit平均提升6.4个百分点准确率（最高12.8pp），100%保持成本单调性，交通数据集的时间价值落在已发表的交通经济学范围内；即使基础模型上下文缩减至10%，仍保持至少6pp的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 该方法在保留经济学可解释性和结构约束的同时，能有效利用基础模型的预测能力，且对基础模型的上下文限制具有稳健性，为融合机器学习与结构经济模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models achieve strong accuracy on choice prediction tasks, but their predictions often violate the economic logic those tasks require: raising a price can increase predicted demand, implied willingness-to-pay estimates are frequently negative or implausible, and unavailable alternatives receive nonzero probability. We propose a two-stage adapter that takes a foundation model's predicted choice probabilities as a precomputed feature and embeds them inside a multinomial logit's utility. In Stage 1, we fit the multinomial logit's structural coefficients by maximum likelihood with sign constraints; in Stage 2, we freeze those coefficients and fit a small neural correction operating on the foundation model's predictions. We prove that this composition exactly preserves the multinomial logit's marginal rate of substitution, so analytically computable value-of-time becomes a mathematical guarantee rather than an empirical accident. Across three datasets and two foundation models, the adapter gains 6.4 percentage points (pp) of test accuracy on average over the multinomial logit and up to 12.8 pp, maintains 100% cost monotonicity, and produces values of time within the published transportation-economics range on the transportation datasets. Performance degrades gracefully under foundation-model context restriction, retaining at least 6 pp of accuracy gain even at 10% of the original foundation-model context.

</details>


### [100] [Optimizing CUDA like a Human: Micro-Profiling Tools as Expert Surrogates for LLM-Based GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26453)
*Jiading Gai,Shuai Zhang,Kaj Bostrom,Jin Huang,Vihang Patil,Haoyang Fang,Bernie Wang,Huzefa Rangwala,George Karypis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelPro 是一个闭环多智能体系统，通过将大语言模型代码生成与硬件剖析器反馈及可插拔瓶颈检测工具集成，自动生成、剖析并迭代优化 GPU 内核代码。在 KernelBench 上实现了 2.42x/4.69x/5.30x 的几何平均加速比，在所有难度级别上均达到最先进性能，并在 MoE 训练内核上超越手工调优的 Triton 1.23x，同时首次在 CUDA 内核编码代理中实现了能效优化（同等速度下能耗降低 11.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型生成的 GPU 内核代码性能远低于专家手写优化版本，主要瓶颈在于缺乏能够利用硬件性能计数器反馈进行迭代优化的闭环系统，且现有方法仅关注速度优化而忽略能效。

Method: (1) 语义反馈算子，将专家启发式编码为可插拔微剖析工具，将原始硬件指标转化为可操作的自然语言指导；(2) 两阶段工具调用架构，基于 roofline 瓶颈分类筛选专业分析工具；(3) 领域适配的 MCTS，包含渐进式扩展、非对称分支、对数奖励校准、死胡同剪枝和搜索记忆；(4) 基于 CUTLASS/CuTe 代码库的自主代码搜索实现直接 CuTe 源码级代码生成。

Result: KernelBench 上 Level 1/2/3 几何平均加速比 2.42x/4.69x/5.30x；VeOmni MoE 训练内核上超越手调 Triton 1.23x；各组件独立显著提升优化质量（微剖析工具 p<0.0001，MCTS 搜索提升 26%，工具编排提升 23%）；同等速度下能耗降低 11.6%。

Conclusion: KernelPro 通过闭环多智能体系统和硬件反馈驱动的迭代优化，在 GPU 内核自动生成中实现了超越现有方法的性能提升，并首次将能效优化纳入 CUDA 内核编码代理的优化目标，确立了新的最先进水平。

Abstract: We present KernelPro, a closed-loop multi-agent system that automatically generates, profiles, and iteratively optimizes GPU kernel code by integrating large language model (LLM) code generation with hardware profiler feedback and pluggable bottleneck detection tools. KernelPro introduces four contributions: (1) a semantic feedback operator that encodes expert heuristics as pluggable micro-profiling tools, transforming raw hardware metrics into actionable natural language guidance; (2) a two-stage tool invocation architecture where roofline-based bottleneck classification filters which specialized analysis tools execute, combining kernel-level (ncu), instruction-level (SASS), and system-level (nsys) profiling; (3) a domain-adapted MCTS with progressive widening, asymmetric branching, log-reward calibration, dead-end pruning, and search memory for cross-iteration learning; and (4) direct CuTe source-level code generation via autonomous code search over the CUTLASS/CuTe codebase. On KernelBench, KernelPro achieves geometric mean speedups of 2.42x/4.69x/5.30x on Levels 1/2/3, establishing state-of-the-art performance across all difficulty levels. On VeOmni's expert-optimized MoE training kernels, KernelPro achieves 1.23x over hand-tuned Triton by generating a from-scratch raw-CUDA+CuTe Hopper WGMMA kernel. Ablation studies demonstrate that each design component independently and significantly improves optimization quality: micro-profiling tools (p < 0.0001 vs raw metrics), MCTS search (26% higher geometric mean vs greedy, p = 0.004), and proactive tool orchestration (23% improvement, p = 0.035). Finally, KernelPro is the first CUDA kernel coding agent to optimize energy efficiency beyond the speed-only focus of prior systems, demonstrating an 11.6% measured energy reduction at matched speed.

</details>


### [101] [Finding the Time to Think: Learning Planning Budgets in Real-Time RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26463)
*Aneesh Muppidi,Firas Darwish,Dylan Cope,João F. Henriques,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在实时强化学习环境中，环境在智能体决策期间持续运行，本文提出了一种基于轻量级门控策略的变延迟实时强化学习方法，通过让智能体选择状态依赖的规划预算来应对时间成本问题，并在多个实时游戏中取得了优于固定预算和启发式基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习假设环境会无限期等待智能体决策，但在实时环境中，决策时间是有成本的。现有方法要么忽视这一时间成本，要么使用固定的规划预算，无法适应不同状态对规划时间的差异化需求。

Method: 提出变延迟实时强化学习框架，智能体在每个决策点自主选择规划时长。具体做法是在规划器之上训练一个轻量级门控策略（gating policy），该策略根据当前状态选择规划预算（即规划时间长短），从而在决策质量与时间成本之间取得平衡。

Result: 在实时Pac-Man、Tetris、Snake、Speed Hex和Speed Go环境中，门控策略优于固定预算和启发式基线方法；该方法还能迁移到智能体与环境运行于不同GPU的真实实时场景中。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级门控策略进行状态依赖的自适应规划预算选择，是处理实时强化学习中决策时间成本问题的有效途径，且具有良好的跨平台迁移能力。

Abstract: Deliberating takes time. In real-time settings, that time is not free. Standard reinforcement learning (RL) sidesteps this as the environment waits indefinitely for the agent's decision. Instead, we study real-time RL environments where the environment progresses while waiting for the agent's action. Building on prior real-time formalizations, we introduce variable-delay real-time RL, where the agent chooses how long to deliberate at each decision point since the environment progresses. For the planning agents we use, the right delay is state-dependent, and naively planning how long to plan can paralyze the agent. We instead approach this setting by training a lightweight gating policy on top of a planner to select state-dependent planning budgets. Across real-time Pac-Man, Tetris, Snake, Speed Hex, and Speed Go, our gating policy outperforms fixed-budget and heuristic baselines, and transfers to a real-time setup where the environment and agent run on two different GPUs.

</details>


### [102] [A Causal Foundation Model for Structure and Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26467)
*Max Zhu,Martino Mansoldo,Ching-Hao Wang,Stefan Groha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-CFM是一个因果基础模型，能够在多个因果问题上同时预测因果结构和结果，支持Pearl因果层次的所有三个层级，并在真实数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果模型通常局限于单一任务（如仅结构学习或仅结果预测），缺乏统一的框架来处理Pearl因果层次中所有层级（关联、干预、反事实）的查询。

Method: 采用基于Prior-Data Fitted Networks（PFN）的架构，在合成数据上进行训练，学习因果结构与结果的联合分布，支持利用已知图结构信息来提升预测性能。

Result: 在真实数据集上，TabPFN-CFM在因果结构预测和结果预测任务上均优于现有的结构学习基线和结果预测基线。

Conclusion: TabPFN-CFM证明了一个统一的因果基础模型可以有效处理多种因果推理任务，且合成数据训练的策略能够泛化到真实世界场景。

Abstract: We introduce TabPFN-CFM, a causal foundation model that can handle multiple causal problems. TabPFN-CFM predicts both causal structure and outcomes from observational data, supports queries on all three levels of Pearl's Causal Hierarchy and uses known graph structure when available to improve predictions. TabPFN-CFM is trained on synthetic datasets, and generalises to real datasets, demonstrating improved performance over both structural and outcome prediction baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Epiphany-Aware KV Cache Eviction Without the Attention Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26472)
*Steven Kolawole,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiKV，一种基于"顿悟分数"（epiphany score）的KV缓存淘汰方法，通过直接读取模型前向传播中的内部表示变化来评估token重要性，无需注意力矩阵、训练或定制内核，在长推理链场景下相比传统注意力权重评分方法更高效，可实现16倍上下文长度扩展，在MATH-500和AIME-2024上达到或超越现有最强基线，同时速度提升达2.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型生成数万token的思维链时，KV缓存成为部署瓶颈。现有基于注意力权重的缓存淘汰方法在长推理轨迹中噪声大，且需物化注意力矩阵，阻碍了生产推理中使用融合内核。

Method: 提出顿悟分数（epiphany score），直接从前向传播中读取模型内部表示的变化来评估token重要性，无需注意力矩阵；采用因果滚动z-score消除位置趋势；支持直接与FlashAttention推理栈集成，无需训练、分类器或定制内核。

Result: 在4096-token缓存下，EpiKV在MATH-500上达72%（ThinKV 71%，H2O 67%）；滞后归一化变体在8192-token缓存的AIME-2024上达37%（最强基线33%），速度提升达2.8倍；上下文长度可扩展至注意力评分方法的16倍。

Conclusion: EpiKV通过轻量级、无训练的内部表示评分机制，在长推理链场景中实现了优于传统注意力权重方法的KV缓存淘汰性能，同时保持高效率和兼容现有推理框架。

Abstract: As reasoning models emit chains of thought tens of thousands of tokens long, KV cache increasingly becomes a deployment bottleneck. Existing cache eviction methods rank tokens by attention weight, which is a noisy importance proxy in long reasoning traces, and prohibits the use of fused kernels in production inference by forcing the model to materialize the attention matrix. In this work, we instead score tokens with a metric we term the epiphany score: the change in the model's internal representation, read directly from the forward pass with no attention matrix and negligible extra state. Our resulting cache eviction method, EpiKV, requires no training, classifier, or custom kernel, and can be used directly in FlashAttention inference stacks unchanged -- scaling to a 16x longer feasible context than attention-based scoring. upper-mid layers negatively) and remove a positional trend with a causal rolling z-score. At a 4096-token cache EpiKV reaches 72% on MATH-500, matching the strongest attention-based baseline (ThinKV 71%, H2O 67%); a lag-normalized KV variant reaches 37% on AIME-2024 at 8192 tokens against the best of them (33%), at up to 2.8x the speed.

</details>


### [104] [When Does Quality-Aware Multimodal Fusion Matter? A Leakage-Safe Diagnostic for Decision-Level Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26473)
*Jaden Moon,Arvind Pillai,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诊断方法，通过排列测试样本的可靠性分数来检验多模态系统是否真正利用这些分数进行推理。实验发现，尽管理论上利用可靠性信息可以提升性能，但现有模型在排列分数后性能并未下降，说明模型实际上并未利用这些分数进行决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许多多模态系统声称通过估计各模态的可靠性来加权贡献，但尚不清楚这些可靠性分数是真正影响模型决策，还是仅仅与性能存在相关性。作者希望区分真正的因果影响与单纯的相关性。

Method: 提出一种简单的诊断方法：在模型训练完成后，固定模型和输入，但在测试时随机排列各样本的可靠性分数。如果模型决策依赖于这些分数，排列应导致性能下降；反之则说明分数未被利用。在StressID（压力识别）和CMU-MOSEI（情感分析）两个数据集上进行实验。

Result: 在StressID和CMU-MOSEI上，排列可靠性分数后模型性能未发生变化，尽管理论上通过选择每个样本的最佳模态可以获得显著的性能提升。但在正控制实验中（即可靠性信号能准确识别正确模态时），相同的融合规则能带来显著改进。

Conclusion: 可靠性信号只有在能够可靠预测单模态正确性时，才会影响融合后的决策；否则，模型虽然计算了可靠性分数，但实际上并未在推理中利用这些信息进行决策。

Abstract: Many multimodal systems estimate the reliability of each modality and weight their contributions to the final prediction. However, it remains unclear whether these scores influence model decisions or merely correlate with performance. We propose a simple diagnostic to test whether reliability information is used during inference. After training, the model and inputs are fixed while reliability scores are permuted across test examples. If predictions depend on these scores, performance should degrade. Experiments on StressID for stress recognition and CMU-MOSEI for sentiment analysis show that permuting reliability scores leaves performance unchanged despite substantial potential gains from selecting the best modality per example. In positive controls where reliability signals identify the correct modality, the same frozen fusion rules yield significant improvements, indicating that reliability signals influence fused decisions only when they reliably predict unimodal correctness.

</details>


### [105] [Localizing RL-Induced Tool Use to a Single Crosscoder Feature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26474)
*Andrii Shportko,Shubham Bhokare,Ahmed Zeyad A Alzahrani,Bowen Cheng,Gustavo Mercier,Jessica Hullman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出专用特征交叉编码器（DFC），通过隔离强化学习引入的紧凑特征集，实现工具调用能力的迁移与运行时行为控制。实验表明，DFC在Qwen2.5-3B上将RL模型的工具正确率提升31.1个百分点，并将工具调用能力被动迁移给冻结的基座模型（能力溢出效应+6.8个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习微调能显著提升语言模型的工具使用等智能体行为，但其内部表征的机制性变化（哪些特征涌现、哪些被保留）尚不清楚，且缺乏无需重训练的行为控制方法。

Method: 提出专用特征交叉编码器（Dedicated Feature Crosscoders, DFC），通过跨编码器超参数扫描（48组），将RL引入的特征与基座特征分离，编码-解码重建后评估工具调用正确率与能力迁移效果。

Result: DFC重建使RL模型工具正确率提升+31.1±9.7个百分点，并向冻结的基座模型被动迁移工具调用能力+6.8±5.0个百分点（能力溢出效应）。

Conclusion: DFC分区能够将RL引入的能力集中到最小、可操控的特征集合中，从而实现对智能体LLM的运行时行为控制，且无需重训练。

Abstract: Fine-tuning through RL reshapes the internal representations of language models to enable agentic behaviors such as tool use, yet the mechanistic basis of these changes remains poorly understood. While RL substantially improves structured tool-call generation, it is unclear which features emerge, which are preserved, and whether identified features can be leveraged for retraining-free behavioral control. In this work, we show that $\textit{Dedicated Feature Crosscoders (DFC)}$ isolate a compact set of RL-specific features that mediate tool-calling capability in $\texttt{Qwen2.5-3B}$. Across a $48$-crosscoder hyperparameter sweep, encode-decode reconstruction improves the RL model's tool correctness by $+31.1 \pm {9.7}$ pp and passively transfers tool-calling ability to the frozen base model by $+6.8 \pm 5.0$ pp which we call a $\textit{capability spillover}$. Our findings show that DFC partitioning concentrates RL-introduced capability into a minimal, steerable feature set that enables runtime behavioral control of agentic LLMs.

</details>


### [106] [Retrieval-Warmed Energy-Based Reasoning: A Five-Arm Ablation Methodology for Diffusion-as-Inference on Structured Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26476)
*Libo Sun,Po-Wei Harn,Zewei Zhang,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于检索热启的能量扩散模型（RW-EBR），提出了一种五臂消融分析方法，将三类混杂效应（类别先验偏移、随机热启、图对齐的值复用）分离，并在连通性检测（Erdős–Rényi）和数独任务上定位了各任务的瓶颈组件——前者是图对齐，后者是键质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热启扩散采样器能加速迭代推理，但增益来源不清楚。现有方法（如IRED扩散模型加Hopfield记忆）混杂了多种效应，缺乏系统的诊断工具来分离与归因。

Method: 提出五臂消融方法：oracle（完整检索）、best-constant（最优常数）、per-query-random（每查询随机初始）、shuffled（打乱图对齐）、aligned（正确对齐）。该方法源自LLM-RAG评估框架，通过逐臂对比分离三类效应：类先验偏移、随机热启、图对齐值复用。

Result: 在连通性-2任务上，aligned与shuffled-oracle之间的平衡准确率差距达+35个百分点，且per-query随机初始化效果低于冷启动，证明图对齐是主导因素而非偏差偏移或随机性。在数独任务上，诊断在键质量检查阶段即止步，得到清晰阴性结果，表明瓶颈不同——是键编码质量。

Conclusion: 五臂消融方法能有效命名各任务的首个阻塞组件：连通性任务中是图对齐（alignment），数独任务中是键质量（key quality）。该工作属于结构化与时空推理范畴，可用于解释扩散采样器失败模式。

Abstract: Warm-started diffusion samplers accelerate iterative inference, but it is rarely clear which part of the pipeline carries the gain. We study \textbf{retrieval-warmed energy-based reasoning (RW-EBR)} -- an IRED energy-based diffusion model \cite{du2024ired} augmented with a Modern Hopfield trajectory memory -- and contribute a \textbf{five-arm ablation methodology} (oracle, best-constant, per-query-random, shuffled, aligned) that separates three confounded effects: class-prior bias shift, stochastic warm-starting, and graph-aligned value reuse. The diagnostic decomposition is adapted from LLM-RAG evaluation \cite{ru2024ragchecker}. On \textbf{connectivity-2} (Erdős--Rényi all-pairs reachability), the aligned-vs-shuffled-oracle swing reaches \textbf{$+35$\,pp} balanced accuracy on a fixed 1{,}000-graph validation-set diagnostic, with value distribution and retrieval mechanics fixed, only per-graph alignment destroyed, while per-query random initialisation falls below cold -- per-graph alignment, not bias shift or stochasticity, dominates. Yet the \emph{deployable} cold-prediction pipeline misses the acceptance gate at stored-value quality. The same diagnostic logic, stopped at the key-quality screen, applied to \textbf{Sudoku} with a task-specific key encoder produces a clean negative at a \emph{different} component -- key quality, under the current setup. The decomposition names the first blocking component on each task. The setting -- graph reachability refined by an iterative diffusion sampler, with explainability of failure modes as the lens -- places the work within structured and spatio-temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [107] [What Survives When You Compress a Recursive Reasoner for the Edge?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26488)
*Pearse Jim,Steven Kolawole,Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Glory Bagai,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 递归推理模型在极端压缩下，全局推理能力会先于局部预测崩溃，但通过每通道校准的INT4量化可以在不重训练的情况下恢复，且提出无标签信号"进位轨迹保真度"可预测这一损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归推理模型能够在仅有几百万参数的情况下解决复杂结构化任务，但部署在边缘硬件上需要大幅压缩。与常规序列模型不同，递归推理的量化误差会跨递归循环累积而非跨输出token累积，导致标准压缩直觉失效，需要系统研究压缩对递归推理能力的影响。

Method: 在全精度扫描、三种任务、两种递归架构下，测试了INT4剪枝、蒸馏、线性注意力等压缩方法，并引入每通道校准INT4量化以及进位轨迹保真度作为预测信号。

Result: 激进压缩保留了局部预测（单元准确率保持）但摧毁了全局推理（谜题准确率归零）；token级目标（包括量化感知训练）无法修复；MLP混合递归架构崩溃但注意力架构在相同任务上不受影响；每通道校准INT4无需重训练即可逆转崩溃。

Conclusion: 提供了部署方案：闪流传嵌入消除99.4MB瓶颈、INT8单周期匹配全深度精度且FLOPs减少6倍（8MB SoC）、校准INT4适配4MB微控制器。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning models can solve complex structured tasks with only a few million parameters by repeatedly updating a latent state. Deploying these models on edge hardware requires significant compression, but unlike conventional sequence models, quantization errors compound across recursive reasoning cycles rather than across output tokens. As a result, standard intuitions about compression fail to apply. In this work, we ask what survives when recursive reasoners are compressed. Across a full precision sweep, three tasks, and two recursive architectures, we find that aggressive compression preserves local prediction but destroys global reasoning: cell accuracy holds while puzzle-exact accuracy collapses to zero under naive INT4 pruning, distillation, and linear attention alike. Token-level objectives, including quantization-aware training, cannot repair it. The collapse is architectural -- it strikes MLP-mixing recursion but not attention on the same task -- and we reverse it with per-channel calibrated INT4 without retraining. We also introduce carry-trajectory fidelity, the cosine similarity to the full-precision reasoning path, as a label-free signal that predicts this damage and its recovery before a task evaluation. The combined result is a deployment recipe: flash-streamed embeddings remove a 99.4MB bottleneck, INT8 at one cycle matches full-depth accuracy at 6x fewer FLOPs (8MB SoC), and calibrated INT4 fits a 4MB microcontroller.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Probabilistic Filters with Strictly Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26497)
*Eviatar Bach,Ricardo Baptista,Jochen Bröcker,Bohan Chen,Andrew Stuart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于严格适当评分规则和Transformer架构的集合数据同化方法（PSEF），通过仅使用合成状态-观测轨迹训练分析映射来近似贝叶斯滤波分布，在非线性、非高斯和多模态后验问题上优于经典方法和基于均方误差的学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯滤波分布是动力系统不确定性量化的自然对象，但作为监督学习目标难以直接获得。作者旨在利用可合成的系统轨迹数据，训练一个能够近似滤波分布的分析映射。

Method: 提出PSEF方法：使用置换不变的Transformer作为分析映射，以预报集合和观测为输入，输出分析集合；采用能量评分（严格适当评分规则）作为训练目标；理论上证明了在可实现性假设下总体目标最小化等价于真实贝叶斯滤波分布。

Result: 数值实验表明：PSEF在非线性、非高斯和多模态后验分布上准确近似滤波分布；数据同化任务中优于经典方法和基于MSE的学习方法；对近高斯问题，学习EnKF校正效果最佳；对高度非高斯问题，摒弃归纳偏置的端到端方法更优。

Conclusion: 严格适当评分规则结合Transformer架构能够有效学习贝叶斯滤波分布，且根据问题的高斯性程度可选择不同的归纳偏置策略。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering of partially and noisily observed dynamical systems seeks to infer the evolving conditional distribution of the state of a dynamical system, given observations, in an online fashion. This Bayesian filtering distribution is the natural object for uncertainty quantification, but it is rarely available as a supervised learning target. However, one can often use the forecast model to generate synthetic system trajectories, along with synthetic observations. We introduce the proper scoring ensemble filter (PSEF), an ensemble data assimilation method based on training an analysis map to approximate the filtering distribution using only synthetic state--observation trajectories. The analysis step is represented as a permutation-invariant, transformer-based map that takes as input a forecast ensemble and observations, producing an analysis ensemble. Training is based on strictly proper scoring rules -- with the energy score used in our implementation -- so that probabilistic accuracy is rewarded over the whole probability distribution. We prove that, under a realizability assumption, the population objective is minimized by the true Bayesian filtering distribution. We also derive the finite-ensemble empirical objective used in training and relate its single state--observation trajectory form to the population objective, using a mean-field consistency argument. Numerical experiments show that the learned filter accurately approximates challenging filtering distributions, including nonlinear, non-Gaussian, and multi-modal posteriors, and achieves stronger performance in data assimilation tasks than classical methods or learning-based methods with mean-squared-error objectives. For close-to-Gaussian problems, learning a correction to the EnKF is the best approach, while for highly non-Gaussian problems an end-to-end approach that discards this inductive bias is superior.

</details>


### [109] [Multipath Adaptive Gated Bottleneck Latent ODE with Raman Data Fusion for Cell Culture Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26520)
*Johnny Peng,Thanh Tung Khuat,Ellen Otte,Katarzyna Musial,Bogdan Gabrys*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合门控瓶颈潜变量常微分方程（GB-Latent ODE）与多路径即时微调（MP-JIT-FT）的自适应框架，并融合拉曼光谱数据，用于哺乳动物细胞培养过程中关键过程参数的早期多日预测。在38批5L生物反应器运行数据上，该方法在9个目标变量中的8个上优于全局Latent ODE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哺乳动物细胞培养过程持续时间长、关键参数易漂移，而测量数据稀疏、采样不规则、运行条件异质，且早期行为相似的批次可能走向不同的未来，导致传统方法难以在早期进行有效的多日预测，无法及时调整补料、采样和控制策略。

Method: 1) GB-Latent ODE：在标准Latent ODE基础上引入可学习的变量级门控和掩码感知瓶颈，压缩高维稀疏输入，提升有限数据下的学习能力。2) MP-JIT-FT：对部分观测到的运行，检索相似历史轨迹，聚类局部邻域为候选模式，为每个模式微调独立模型生成多条可能路径，并给出基于重建的置信度分数。3) 拉曼光谱融合：通过机器学习软传感器将密集拉曼光谱转换为伪观测值，丰富稀疏的离线测量数据。

Result: 在覆盖14种条件的38批5L补料批次生物反应器运行数据上，MP-JIT-FT结合拉曼融合在9个目标变量中的8个上优于全局Latent ODE基线，取得最佳平均排名。多路径增益在局部相似前缀发散时最大，拉曼融合在早期动力学能代表后期行为时最有效。

Conclusion: 该自适应框架有效解决了生物过程预测中数据稀疏、运行异质和路径分叉等挑战，通过多路径预测和拉曼数据融合显著提升了早期预测的准确性和鲁棒性，为及时的过程干预提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Mammalian cell-culture processes underpin the manufacture of many biopharmaceuticals, yet keeping a run on track is hard: critical process parameters drift over days, and an off-specification trend is often confirmed too late to intervene. Early-stage, multi-day forecasts could enable timely adjustment of feeding, sampling, and control, but bioprocess forecasting is challenging because measurements are sparse and irregularly sampled, operating conditions are heterogeneous across cell lines and media, and runs with near-identical early behaviour can diverge into different futures. We propose an adaptive framework combining a Gated Bottleneck Latent Ordinary Differential Equation (GB-Latent ODE) with Multi-Path Just-In-Time Fine Tuning (MP-JIT-FT). The GB-Latent ODE augments the stan dard Latent ODE with learnable variable-wise gating and a mask-aware bottleneck that compress high-dimensional sparse inputs, improving learning under limited data. Given a partially observed run, MP-JIT-FT retrieves similar historical trajectories, clusters the local neighbourhood into candidate regimes, and fine-tunes a separate model per regime to produce multiple plausible paths, each with a reconstruction-based confidence score, not a single averaged forecast. We further fuse Raman spectroscopy data: a machine-learning soft sensor turns dense Raman spectra into pseudo-observations that enrich the sparse offline measurements for more robust training. On 38 fed-batch 5L bioreactor runs spanning 14 conditions, MP-JIT-FT with Raman fusion achieves the best average rank and outperforms a global Latent ODE baseline on 8 of 9 target variables. Using local-divergence metrics, we show the multi-path gains are largest when locally similar prefixes diverge, whereas Raman fusion helps most when early dynamics are representative of later behaviour.

</details>


### [110] [Theory-Scale Auto-Formalization of Logics for Computer Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26525)
*Yuming Feng,Frederick Pu,One An,Osbert Bastani,Li Zhang,Jiani Huang,Xujie Si,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LCS-Bench，一个基于《计算机科学逻辑》教材的大规模理论级自动形式化基准，涵盖327个教材条目、4076条Lean声明和超过85000行Lean代码。通过半自动化智能体流水线构建，并引入定义等价性检查器实现细粒度评估。对14个模型的评测表明，该基准具有高质量和挑战性，SOTA模型在自动形式化任务上仅达20.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化工作主要集中于孤立语句，而理论级的自动形式化——即一致性地翻译数百个相互依赖的定义、引理和定理——由于在一致性、忠实性、可扩展性和正确性方面的挑战，仍然是一个开放问题。因此需要构建一个专门的理论级自动形式化基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: (1) 提出LCS-Bench基准，基于《计算机科学逻辑》教材； (2) 设计半自动化智能体流水线，包括概念图、形式签名规划、问题追踪、sorry填充与反例搜索，辅以人类专家忠实性审查； (3) 引入数据引擎自动衍生五个评测轨道； (4) 提出新颖的评估协议，包含定义等价性检查器，实现更细粒度和更忠实的评估。

Result: (1) LCS-Bench质量高、一致且忠实； (2) 基准具有高度挑战性，SOTA模型在自动形式化任务上仅达20.1%； (3) 分析揭示了理论级自动形式化的关键发现，并指出了有前景的未来方向。

Conclusion: 理论级自动形式化仍极具挑战性，LCS-Bench为相关研究提供了高质量、多维度的评估平台，当前SOTA性能远未饱和，未来工作可从提升模型的形式推理能力和跨理论一致性等方面展开。

Abstract: Auto-formalization is critical for scalable formal verification, but existing progress largely focuses on isolated statements, while theory-scale auto-formalization, which coherently translates hundreds of interdependent definitions, lemmas, and theorems, remains open due to challenges in consistency, faithfulness, scalability, and correctness. In this paper, we introduce LCS-Bench, a stand-alone, theory-scale benchmark based on Logics for Computer Science. LCS-Bench is built through a novel semi-automated agentic pipeline that leverages concept graphs, formal signature planning, issue tracking, sorry-filling with counter-example search, complemented by faithfulness review from human experts. The resulting artifact covers 327 textbook items, over 4,076 Lean declarations, and more than 85K lines of Lean code.
